# Rank8Damage6 — complete Director conference record

This document contains full selected procedures/shared clauses, all submitted questions and current answers, and all new main/branch cases. Source histories are also preserved unchanged in ORIGINAL_HISTORIES.json and the original ZIP.

# Rank8Damage6 — complete proposed procedures
Video-game-only authored proposal. New scoped completions are labelled NEW. None is installed in the project. Native sources and the precise decision history accompany the model.
## Deep Crust Spike
### Actual access
| Class | Rank | Minimum class level | School | Base RP |
|---|---:|---:|---|---:|
| Druid | 8 | 15 | Transmutation | 21 |
| Mage | 8 | 15 | Transmutation | 21 |
| Wizard | 8 | 15 | Transmutation | 21 |
### Complete procedure
DEEP CRUST SPIKE — NEW RD8-SPIKE-02; preserves 10d8 total and both printed types.
ACCESS / CAST: Druid, Mage or Wizard with actual rank-8 Transmutation access, minimum class level15; one Action, base21RP, V/S/F; instantaneous, no continuing concentration. Apply all routed RD8 shared rules.
SELECTION / UPWARD ORIGIN: Choose one actually perceived creature with an actual current body/occupied volume, and a genuinely known upward-facing solid supporting surface below it. No creature type is excluded by its name. NEW single-target completion: choose an actual point on that surface directly below an intersection with the target's current body in the scene's real gravity frame; at most30 feet of unobstructed upward separation is allowed. Both selected origin and the body witness must be within120 feet of the caster's actual source origin. Require actual caster-to-origin line of effect and origin-to-the-same-body-witness vertical line of effect. The source point must be perceivable/known by an actually sufficient sense, not a guessed hidden floor. Ordinary solid floors, ground, ice, decks or supported platforms can supply the surface; there is no invented stone-only requirement. A ceiling overhead is not a below-origin. Water or open air alone supplies no solid surface, but a genuinely perceived solid bottom with an unobstructed path through actual transparent water can qualify. A floating creature can be struck within the stated30-foot vertical reach; flight is not blanket immunity. A floor, closed hull or intervening body/terrain blocker is not penetrated. No subterranean depth, minimum crust thickness or tunneling is manufactured.
This is a targeted eruption, not a damaging cylinder: no other creature is hit merely for crossing the line or standing nearby. The target, selected origin and geometry are fixed for the event. Revalidate the same target and origin after interruption; no retarget, alternate floor or favorable origin shift. A known missing surface, excessive gap or cover fails preflight without payment; a genuinely changed late predicate after a paid legal commitment prevents that payload without refund. Ordinary Ready chooses these target-dependent facts at release.
RESOLUTION: One DEX save against the actual class DC. Roll five d8 fire and five d8 piercing, exactly10d8 total. Failure takes both complete typed pools; success takes floor(fire/2) and floor(piercing/2). Apply RD8-MIXED-02, current defenses and one grouped damage event/root. Immunity to one type does not erase the other; no attack roll, crit, vulnerability bypass or rider is implied. Actual resulting Wounds, zero/death, concentration and body lifecycle resolve normally.
END / PHYSICAL RESULT: The lava spike is a transient magical manifestation that ends with this event. No real platform, spear, tunnel, terrain cost, harvestable lava, persistent fire, structural/object damage, forced movement, knock-up, restraint, Prone or additional Falling packet is granted. It changes no gear custody or anatomy beyond independently earned Core consequences. Later suppression/dispel cannot refund completed damage; before/at manifestation suppression follows RD8-ENDINGS-02.
Applicable full shared clauses: RD8-CAST-02, RD8-GEOMETRY-02, RD8-DAMAGE-02, RD8-MIXED-02, RD8-INFORMATION-02, RD8-ENDINGS-02.
## Molecular Destabilization
### Actual access
| Class | Rank | Minimum class level | School | Base RP |
|---|---:|---:|---|---:|
| Druid | 8 | 15 | Transmutation | 21 |
| Mage | 8 | 15 | Transmutation | 21 |
| Wizard | 8 | 15 | Transmutation | 21 |
### Complete procedure
MOLECULAR DESTABILIZATION — NEW RD8-MOLECULAR-02.
ACCESS / CAST: Druid, Mage or Wizard with actual rank-8 Transmutation access, minimum class level15; Action, base21RP, V/S/F,120 feet; instantaneous, no maintained concentration. Apply the routed shared rules.
TARGET: One actually perceived existing creature with an actual current body and caster-to-body line of effect. Living, nonliving, finite summoned and genuinely transformed creatures are not excluded merely by those labels. Use their actual present body and source-specific defenses. A corpse, unattended item, building, false image or absent/ended creature is not a creature target; no object-damage extension or anatomy scanner is supplied. Known failure prevents commitment; late loss of the same target/line/source gives the paid attempt no effect and no substitution.
RESOLUTION: One CON save against actual spell DC. Roll exactly10d6 force. Failure takes the total, success floor(total/2); force immunity/resistance/prevention and real sharing apply normally. This is a save-based event, not a critical or armor-piercing attack. Caster owns the one damage root; actual transfers retain it. Finish actual damage/lifecycle, Wounds, death or zero-HP replacements and concentration.
PARTIAL DISSOLUTION: This is presentation of the printed damage, not an additional Disintegrate rule. It does not automatically remove a limb, reduce size, erase equipment, destroy remains, prohibit reconstruction, override immortality, grant a soul or reset a body. A living zero-HP body is not therefore dead. An ended finite familiar may leave no remains because its own actual source says so; a real dead body's surviving gear and matter follow its existing lifecycle. A form ending uses the same creature and actual anatomy-placement/gear rules, not a fresh full-HP body. Null applies to damage only and does not keep an independently expired or dismissed source alive. No ongoing dissolve tick or dispellable post-event debuff remains.
Applicable full shared clauses: RD8-CAST-02, RD8-GEOMETRY-02, RD8-DAMAGE-02, RD8-INFORMATION-02, RD8-ENDINGS-02.
## Soul Pyre
### Actual access
| Class | Rank | Minimum class level | School | Base RP |
|---|---:|---:|---|---:|
| Cleric | 8 | 15 | Necromancy | 21 |
| Druid | 8 | 15 | Necromancy | 21 |
| Wizard | 8 | 15 | Necromancy | 21 |
### Complete procedure
SOUL PYRE — NEW RD8-PYRE-02; explicitly8d6 total, split4d6 fire plus4d6 necrotic.
ACCESS / CAST: Cleric, Druid or Wizard with actual rank-8 Necromancy access, minimum class level15; Action, base21RP, V/S and the actual M/F alternative. No priced reagent is invented. A reusable focus can satisfy the focus alternative but cannot supply the real corpse that fuels the effect. Instantaneous; no continuing concentration outside a held Ready.
CORPSE / OWNERSHIP: Select one actually perceived, physically present principal corpse/remains body record, whose genuine death and origin are recorded and which is currently available and not already consumed or disallowed as a resource by its own source. NEW eligibility completion: one retained principal body identity is the unit, regardless of size, prior creature type or age; a severed part, pile assembled from fragments or duplicate reference is not another principal body. Ordinary missing-part history or skeletal principal remains does not alone prohibit it. No minimum body mass, percentage, humanoid-only, necromancy-only or arbitrary age cutoff is added. There must actually be one qualifying principal corpse now; unresolved global decay cannot be assumed to supply one. A Dying0/Stable0 living body is not eligible. No phantom body, copied form, already-consumed standard animated body or expired/destroyed finite summon supplies new fuel. A genuine Ranger beast corpse or a genuinely reconstructed body later dying can qualify only through its own actual current body history and permissions; no owner-level/CR test applies. This spell needs no consent from a deceased body's former controller, but neither transfers legal ownership of surviving gear nor asserts ownership/finality of any soul.
GEOMETRY: NEW separation of the two20-foot values: the corpse's fixed current body-origin/anchor must be within20 feet of the caster source, with genuine perception and line of effect. On effect resolution its actual current anchor is the center of a20-foot-radius closed3D sphere. Select all actual creatures with body intersection and center-to-the-same-witness line of effect, including the caster, allies and carrier. No favorable exclusions, objects or structural damage are implied. Real corpse inventory survives as described below. An actual acquired initial-area exclusion, such as the Wizard's Evocation-only benefit, applies only if its exact prerequisites hold; Soul Pyre is Necromancy and gains no Evocation permission by analogy.
ATOMIC FUEL TRANSACTION: NEW spell-specific choice: the corpse is selected but neither reserved nor consumed during commitment or its counter window. At resolution revalidate that same identity at its actual current anchor, still within range/LoE, source-eligible and available. A move that still satisfies all predicates moves the eventual blast center; an out-of-range move, prior consumption, actual return to life or other invalidation prevents the paid effect without a substitute or refund. Resolve simultaneous consumers in the established neutral authoritative event order. At the valid effect checkpoint atomically claim and mark this one body consumed, snapshot blast geometry/recipients and create the blast event. No later consumer can claim its old corpse; a previously provisional Auto-Raise reservation confers no priority over actual lawful consumption. A countered spell consumes no corpse. For Ready the actual corpse is selected/rechecked at release; there is no preparation-time corpse lock. This is not a global change to printed consumed spell materials.
DAMAGE: Each admitted creature makes a DEX save. One shared raw pool of four d6 fire and four d6 necrotic serves this blast. Failure takes each full type; success halves each type with floor before RD8-MIXED-02 and actual defenses. Separate recipient roots share the original caster as dealer, not the dead body's former owner. It remains one original-recipient event per creature, with source-qualified transfers, temp HP, life and concentration. Absence, immunity or suppression at a recipient does not refund consumed fuel or give a second blast. If the creating manifestation itself is wholly prevented at the fuel/source checkpoint, consumption does not begin. Zero admitted damage recipients still leaves a lawfully executed corpse-burning effect, but creates no unnecessary damage RNG or roots.
REMAINS / END: NEW physical completion: burn the principal body into spent ordinary ash/char at its actual location, retaining the same consumed-body lineage and missing-part/corpse history. Never create a second corpse/fuel/loot entitlement by splitting ashes. Surviving worn, carried or lodged real items are not consumed or automatically damaged by the spell; detach them locally at their actual positions under ordinary support/fall rules, preserving IDs and provenance. No remote deposit or inventory deletion. Actual non-damage material consumption is not converted by Null. Later dispel cannot restore the corpse or refund costs. A separate reconstruction/resurrection source must independently support these actual spent remains, its costs and restrictions; this spell grants neither such a recovery nor a metaphysical ban on it. Soul Pyre supplies no soul capture, RP, healing or resource-return rider.
Applicable full shared clauses: RD8-CAST-02, RD8-GEOMETRY-02, RD8-DAMAGE-02, RD8-MIXED-02, RD8-INFORMATION-02, RD8-ENDINGS-02.
## Incendiary Cloud
### Actual access
| Class | Rank | Minimum class level | School | Base RP |
|---|---:|---:|---|---:|
| Mage | 8 | 15 | Evocation | 21 |
| Wizard | 8 | 15 | Evocation | 21 |
### Complete procedure
INCENDIARY CLOUD — NEW RD8-CLOUD-02, explicitly resolving the Instantaneous/per-round conflict.
ACCESS / CAST: Mage or Wizard with actual rank-8 Evocation access, minimum class level15; Action, base21RP, V/S/F. NEW chosen ongoing behavior: concentration up to60 seconds from actual establishment, not an instantaneous single hit. Select one actually perceived center within20 feet of the caster with line of effect; create a fixed20-foot-radius closed3D sphere. The source range20 and printed radius20 are independently retained. No free movement of the center or tether to the moving caster is created.
PULSES: NEW express extension of P20-CLOCKS-01 and P20-FAMILY-EXPOSURE-01 only to this identity. Attempt a pulse at establishment t0 and t0+6,+12,...,+54 seconds. Expiry at t0+60 wins at equality; no final+60 pulse. These source-owned events require no caster/recipient/companion turn and grant none. At each pulse recompute actual creature/body intersection and center-to-witness line of effect, then freeze its eligible exposure set before damage. Include caster and allies. Entry, exit, reentry, forced movement, an extra turn, birth-round companion phase or suppression ending supplies no extra pulse. Entering between pulses is not immediate damage; genuine absence at a pulse means no exposure.
The per-recipient Incendiary Cloud family ledger permits at most one admitted cloud exposure per numbered round across all owners/casts. Outside initiative use the scene's continuing six-second exposure interval fixed before the exposure, preserving spent history when mapped into combat; changing timing mode never refreshes it. Earliest actually admitted opportunity claims the family slot; simultaneous eligible sources use established application order then stable source identity. Save, immunity or later prevention after admission still consumes it. Wholly suppressed or geometrically ineligible expressions do not claim it, so another actually eligible cloud later that round can act. Retain each cast's separate concentration, source/pulse history and original deadline; no strongest-damage reroll/selection. This ledger is new for this family only, not a universal area cap.
RESOLUTION: At each actual authorized exposure, DEX save against that cloud's caster DC; failure10d8 fire, success floor(total/2). One fresh shared10d8 pool per pulse with at least one admitted recipient. Apply current real defenses and one distinct root/event per recipient; original caster's current Null flag is sampled anew at each pulse, expressly not inherited from cloud creation. No attack/crit, smoke poison, Burn rider or additional recoil. Real concentrating recipients make their real concentration saves after damage. Complete actual lifecycle before later events. A source can be torn down during a pulse by the normal consequences, but the already-snapshotted pulse is not resampled to erase or add recipients; its initially fixed pulse event resolves, then its future pulses cease. This is a NEW cloud-only batch completion for that already-created event, not a delay of unrelated concentration consequences.
ACTUAL CLASS FEATURES: A Wizard's qualifying Evocation initial-area exclusion can omit its one actual chosen creature only at the creation pulse; it does not mark that body immune to later pulses. Mage Focus/Surge and other abilities apply only through their actual eligible use; the spell grants no free chosen-element casts or extra concentration. An extinguished target or vanished summon is not recreated at later ticks.
PHYSICS / SUPPRESSION: This is a visible, translucent magical fire-and-cinder field, not a borrowed physical-gas stat block. Its appearance changes no mechanical illumination state; existing actual lighting/senses determine visibility. It creates no heavy/light obscuration, blindness, breathable-air removal, smoke, wind transport, water extinguishing rule, material ignition, residual fuel, difficult terrain or object damage. Real existing fog, walls, water, wind and sources continue under their own actual rules. A real opaque blocker prevents its relevant rays; it is not destroyed to reach bodies beyond it. Lesser Antimagic suppresses only its actual clipped intersecting expression, with no paused clock or catch-up. The fixed source's center is not a new emitter creature. A paid cast whose actual creation is suppressed establishes no banked cloud. During a valid ongoing cloud, covered recipient portions do not produce qualifying hits unless an actual unsuppressed intersection witness remains under the accepted suppressor geometry. Caster later entering antimagic does not alone erase a remote valid field. Concentration end, source removal or deadline ends the field and all future pulses; completed damage and independently earned real life states remain.
Applicable full shared clauses: RD8-CAST-02, RD8-GEOMETRY-02, RD8-DAMAGE-02, RD8-INFORMATION-02, RD8-ENDINGS-02.
## Dimensional Break
### Actual access
| Class | Rank | Minimum class level | School | Base RP |
|---|---:|---:|---|---:|
| Cleric | 8 | 15 | Universal | 21 |
| Mage | 8 | 15 | Universal | 21 |
| Wizard | 8 | 15 | Universal | 21 |
### Complete procedure
DIMENSIONAL BREAK — NEW RD8-BREAK-02, force packet plus actual temporary exile.
ACCESS / CAST: Cleric, Mage or Wizard with actual rank-8 Universal access, minimum class level15; Action, base21RP, V/S/F,120 feet. Choose one actually perceived creature the caster designates as an enemy from its own knowledge, with ordinary source-to-body line of effect; this grants no hostility scan. NEW target completion: that same creature is the recipient of both printed components; there is no unprinted force area. Actual absence, corpse or false-image noncreature eligibility does not become a target. Independent transport prevention does not make the otherwise legal force target invalid.
DURATION / COMMITMENT: NEW resolution of Core Instantaneous: the8d10 force is instantaneous, but this cast requires concentration at commitment for its prospective exile. Old incompatible concentration ends even if this cast is countered or its exile later fails. An established cell lasts no more than60 seconds from that establishment; effect expiry is not extended by saves, suppression or return conflict. If no exile establishes, end this cast's unused concentration burden after its pending resolution. A wholly countered cast grants neither component and refunds nothing. An ordinary Ready takes one preparation/counter transaction and selects the actual enemy at legal release; this is not the copied-feat allowance rule.
FORCE FIRST: Roll one DEX save and exactly8d10 force; failure full total, success floor(total/2). Resolve genuine defenses, source-qualified transfer, temp HP, Core/Null life changes, any real concentration/death replacements and response windows. Caster owns its one original damage root. Force immunity or zero actual HP loss does not inherently prevent exile. Conversely a transport bar or spent hard-removal allowance does not retroactively cancel damage. A damage recipient created by transfer is not the exile target. Actual death/removal before exile means no creature to exile; a living Dying0/Stable0 or an extant source-qualified NullDisabled body is not automatically a corpse, and remains eligible if its actual lifecycle permits transport. Do not manufacture immediate death from zero HP.
EXILE SECOND: Recheck the same original creature, surviving generating source/concentration, current range/perception/LoE, actual transport/custody predicates and one-live-cell eligibility. No retarget, second RP/cast or extra force packet. Before the exile CHA save record the target's actual ACCEPT/RESIST intent, using an authorized controller/actual creature decision; inability to consent is RESIST, not guessed willingness. ACCEPT may voluntarily fail or roll; a failed rolled ACCEPT remains voluntary transport. RESIST uses the actual CHA save. Success ends only the exile attempt. A legal failed/voluntary-failed admission consumes the caster-to-target hard-removal allowance only on actual successful establishment; blocked admission consumes no new hard-control history and has no fallback stun/control. No Escape blocks voluntary ACCEPT transportation only; teleport-only restrictions do not silently block forced extraplanar removal, while an actual Dimensional Anchor endpoint predicate does. Preserve each named source's scope. If an already-known bar makes exile impossible, do not fabricate a diagnostic save/root merely to reveal it; resolve the force and silently no exile.
EXPLICIT CELL EXTENSION: RD8-BREAK-CELL-02 expressly extends BAN-CELL-03, BAN-ADMIT-03, BAN-CLOSE-03, BAN-TRANSPORT-CLASS-02, BAN-CONFLICT-04 and BAN-PLACEMENT-HANDOFF-03 to this spell's exile, with only the stated rank8/range120/force-first differences. This is an added named user, not a global teleport/cosmology rule. It shares the one-live spell-cell primary-admission slot with ordinary Banishment: no refresh, replacement or nested second primary cell for the same creature. A Break already targeting such a creature through an actually lawful path can still resolve its force; the slot bars only a second exile. This reciprocal slot check is an explicit new integration requirement, not a claim that old Banishment code already checks Break.
At admission snapshot actual origin plane as an opaque existing reference, fixed world anchor/orientation and current body/gear. It creates an unnamed ephemeral cell, not a chosen known plane or a traversable gate. Primary center is local(0,0), floor z0, original orientation, with5-foot free clearance around its current body on each side and above; round full enclosure dimensions up to5-foot increments. The floor supports the admitted body and the cell provides ordinary nonharmful illumination and an appropriate nonharmful respiration medium. It is not a source of harvested air, floor, material or loot. Its opaque boundary blocks ordinary sight, sound and line of effect. No view of an external hidden destination is granted.
Creature remains able to act, move, concentrate, use actual resources, receive scheduled saves/death checks/recharges and undergo real damage/life consequences on the same advancing clock. Exile is not unconsciousness, healing, an extra turn or time stop. Only actual carried/worn noncreature possessions within real custody/capacity travel with the primary. Mounts, passengers and carried creatures do not ride free; retain the exact separability/physical restraint checks, and fail the transport atomically if required genuine separation is impossible. Dropped gear stays where it actually belongs.
Visitors require their own actual legal source, destination knowledge, path/fit and costs under BAN-ADMIT-03; this spell grants none. Voluntary form change/internal creation must fit the current cell before any later permitted adjustment. Mandatory actual body/life changes still happen, and only then extend the cell just enough to contain the extant bodies plus the required clearance. Secondary bodies retain stable admission and projected origin-anchor metadata. No new attack profile, rating or companion turn is created.
ENDING / RETURN: Deadline, concentration/source end, actual valid dispel or suppression of the exile application closes it permanently once; suppression is not a pause/resume. The caster entering an unrelated remote antimagic field is not itself suppression of this cell's actual application. Primary legal escape also closes the cell but does not drag the escaped creature back. Remaining actual bodies/corpses/objects return; vanished, consumed or escaped things are not recreated. The gate image is not an extra transport route.
Use the complete copied BAN closure/placement authority: return remaining extant entities to the snapshotted origin plane before physical fitting; primary first then secondary creatures by stable admission, actual gear with carriers and loose surviving objects by their projected current local offset. The origin anchor does not move with a later vehicle. NEW expressly inherited terminal-only exception ignores a true endpoint transport-prevention predicate solely for mandatory cell closure, never physical occupancy/walls/anatomy or a future voluntary journey. Try actual current body at its anchor, then a legal squeeze, then nearest legal adjacent placement in the same unobstructed region by minimum displacement, greatest overlap, fixed neutral board order. No remote search, wall crossing, occupied-space passage, healing or fresh turn.
If no fit exists, the cell still ends and the same entity is on its origin plane with canonical placementConflict and anchor metadata, no fictional overlapping footprint or invented universal Action ban. Ordinary conditions, clocks, exposure, life and lawful displacement permissions continue under that interface. Resolve only when a genuinely legal placement becomes available; no reserved sanctuary. Evaluate Falling only after a legal current footprint actually lacks support. Corpses return as corpses, dropped gear is not restored, and the force damage is never undone.
Applicable full shared clauses: RD8-CAST-02, RD8-GEOMETRY-02, RD8-DAMAGE-02, RD8-INFORMATION-02, RD8-ENDINGS-02.
## Illusory Wall of Doom
### Actual access
| Class | Rank | Minimum class level | School | Base RP |
|---|---:|---:|---|---:|
| Wizard | 8 | 15 | Illusion | 21 |
### Complete procedure
ILLUSORY WALL OF DOOM — NEW RD8-WALL-02; preserves one full8d6psychic/Prone collapse.
ACCESS / CAST: Wizard only through its actual rank-8 Illusion access, minimum class level15; Action, base21RP, V/S/F,120 feet; concentration up to60 seconds from establishment. No additional school/class permission is created. NEW event choice: one immediate collapse when the spell manifests, followed by its finite visual aftermath, not ten free attacks or repeated knockdowns.
GEOMETRY: Choose a perceived legal base midpoint on a real solid supporting surface within120 feet, with caster line of effect, and a horizontal forward direction in the scene's actual gravity frame. NEW bounded huge wall:60-foot width,30-foot height, no physical thickness. Its local coordinates are u along its width, v forward, w up; initial image occupies u in[-30,30],v=0,w in[0,30]. The collapsing image sweeps the closed quarter-cylinder |u|<=30, v>=0,w>=0,v*v+w*w<=900. It is not a full30-foot sphere, infinite plane or crushing object. A target body must intersect that swept region at a point having line of effect from the base midpoint; actual cover blocks it. Evaluate true current occupied space, not token center alone. Bodies behind the wall, wholly beyond width/arc or wholly behind real cover are not admitted. Real terrain can clip presentation/line of effect; it is not removed or supplied by the image. A moving ship does not move this fixed source's later visual anchor.
PERCEPTION / ADMISSION: NEW wall-specific visible shock model: at the one collapse checkpoint, each geometrically eligible creature must actually visually perceive some of the manifested wall through its real sensory path and not be prevented from receiving this entire illusion effect by an actual source-specific immunity. Include caster/allies when eligible. Blinded or wholly nonvisual observation is insufficient; no sound, echolocation substitute or free sight through real fog is supplied. Mere creature type, low Intelligence or an assumed mindless label confers no unprinted immunity. True Seeing can identify the wall as false personally, but that identification alone does not block this expressly authored independent psychic/balance payload. This is a selected wall-specific consequence consistent with the actual True Seeing limit on independent psychic effects, NOT an automatic inheritance of Invisible Tide's nonvisual gate. An actual effect that prevents the entire admitted illusion may bar both components; retain its exact scope. Do not reveal hidden exclusions or recipient lists.
ONE COLLAPSE: Snapshot eligible observers and geometry once, even if subsequent damage changes their senses/position. Each makes an INT save against actual Wizard DC (including an acquired Illusion-school bonus). Roll one shared8d6 psychic pool. Failure takes full psychic damage and, after actual damage/life/concentration responses, receives actual Prone if the original recipient still exists in a posture-capable state and is not genuinely immune/prevented. Success takes floor(total/2) and receives no new Prone. Psychic resistance/immunity alone never cancels the separately failed-save Prone; Prone immunity alone never cancels psychic damage. Neither component creates an attack roll, Frightened, stun, restraint, Wound bonus, falling masonry or a hard-denial allowance claim. Existing Prone is not doubled. A real death/survival/form-change consequence is resolved before evaluating that body/rider, with no target substitution.
POSTURE / AFTERMATH: Actual Prone uses ordinary movement/standing restrictions; a flying nonhovering unsupported recipient begins real Falling under the actual schedule, not an instant invented crash packet. A real hover/support source can prevent that fall without removing the Prone state unless it expressly does so. Standing costs the real floor(half current Speed) movement with Speed>0 and sufficient movement; no duration-based free stand. Visual disbelief, later dispel or ended concentration does not refund damage, remove a real earned Wound or stand a body up.
The fallen wall persists only as a bounded visual aftermath inside the same declared region until the original concentration deadline. It is not real rubble, cover, collision, support, difficult terrain, loot or an adaptive intelligent display. An observer with an actual visual path can use one Search/Investigation Action against the original spell DC; success personally identifies it as false. Unambiguous physical contradiction also personally identifies it; True Seeing does so automatically within its actual limits. Identification changes only that observer's treatment of the visual. No new observer, reentry, movement across the image, extra turn, resumed suppression or successful investigation repeats damage/Prone. A new independently paid lawful cast is a new event with real cost and concentration replacement; there is no free reroll by reposing the old wall.
SUPPRESSION / END: Suppression at the initial manifestation/recipient locus prevents only that actual admitted effect, without banking a collapse. If an initially valid source later loses concentration during its already-snapshotted collapse, complete that created collapse batch then remove its own visual aftermath; other concentration consequences are immediate. This NEW one-event batch completion is confined to this wall. Later partial suppression hides its intersecting visual only and consumes no new collapse; the deadline keeps advancing. Original expiry, concentration loss or real removal ends all remaining image, with no repair of genuine changes.
Applicable full shared clauses: RD8-CAST-02, RD8-GEOMETRY-02, RD8-DAMAGE-02, RD8-INFORMATION-02, RD8-ENDINGS-02.
# Complete shared clauses
Only the listed six source procedures route these clauses; the mixed clause applies only to Deep Crust Spike and Soul Pyre.
## RD8-CAST-02
SELECTED for these six ordinary spells only. Keep every complete native classAccess row: all six are rank 8, base 21 RP, minimum listed class level 15; an actual acquired/prepared repertoire entry is required. There is no new upcast, cost waiver, school access or component substitute. Deep Crust Spike, Molecular Destabilization, Incendiary Cloud, Dimensional Break and Illusory Wall of Doom require one Action and V/S/F. Soul Pyre uses one Action and V/S plus the actual Core M/F alternative, without an invented priced or consumed reagent; its real corpse is indispensable effect fuel under RD8-PYRE-02. A focus cannot substitute for that body.
Direct cast preflight checks the actual Action, components, permitted source, declared mode, legal selection, range, perception and line of effect. Known invalid selection causes no commitment or cost. Pay the actual final RP/material/other real costs and establish required concentration before the actual single casting-interruption window. Dissonance is read before payment: limit max(4,2+PB+max(0,casting modifier)), surcharge +1/+2/+3 at limit/limit+3/limit+6, ordinary ranked casting barred at limit+6 without its actual explicit exception. This rank contributes base 2 plus same-name Refrain +1/+2/+3 capped, subject only to the real class's protected rules. First prepared Wizard family use retains its actual base-risk exemption; Mage Focus/chain, Druid and Cleric engines keep their real triggers. A miss, save, counter or late no-effect outcome refunds nothing and never restores displaced concentration. Spell DC is 8+PB+actual casting modifier plus actual acquired modifiers; saves are binary, not critical successes or degrees.
Ordinary Ready is unchanged. At preparation fix spell/non-target mode, provide components, pay and record that one cast, take its counter window and hold concentration. Select the legal creature/corpse/point and target-dependent orientation at release, after the completed perceived trigger, spending the actual Reaction and rechecking current eligibility. No second RP/components/cadence/counter window is created. Soul Pyre's fuel identity is selected at release like its actual target, not reserved or consumed at preparation; this is the explicitly selected spell-specific fuel transaction, not a new general material rule. Ready does not require reserving a corpse already present at preparation: actual non-target casting components and a legal Ready setup are required then; no valid corpse at a later trigger means no release Reaction, and the paid hold keeps only its original deadline. The held spell expires at next regular own START or its actual earlier limit. Effect clocks begin on establishment at release. COPY-READY-ALLOWANCE-02 concerns the seven copied-feat profiles, not these ordinary casts; this cohort receives no feat allowance charge.
## RD8-GEOMETRY-02
NEW express adoption for these six of DC25's continuous 3D Euclidean, closed-volume geometry and frozen-event recipient selection, not a global replacement of historical grids. A scene supplies actual body volumes, source origins, sensory paths, support, opaque barriers and stable entity order. Range is evaluated from the actual caster origin to the selected point or actual creature/body witness according to the local procedure; a convenient visual/center point outside a body never substitutes for a legal witness. At a manifestation freeze its actual geometry and eligible recipients before consequences; later cover destruction, body movement or death does not add recipients to that same event. Recheck the original identities when each pending component executes; no replacement target is selected. Closed volume/body boundary contact counts for these area spells, including large bodies whose centers lie outside. The same intersection witness must have the specified origin line of effect. Total cover blocks; no floor/wall penetration or hidden-entity scan is granted. Private engine eligibility may determine non-admission without revealing its cause. Source-specific component immunity and genuine lesser-antimagic expression suppression precede creation of a damage application. A geometrically missing or wholly suppressed recipient produces no root or save. A spell does not affect an inanimate object merely because a creature is holding it, unless its own procedure expressly specifies non-damage corpse fuel or custody cleanup.
## RD8-DAMAGE-02
Reuse actual Core, class, condition, sharing and Null interfaces in their accepted scope; NEW exact root mapping for these six: the original caster is the dealer. Each genuinely admitted original recipient gets one root. For these area events, after the event's actual geometry/admission/family checks and before any recipient consequences, create all of its admitted recipient roots using the actual original-caster Null state at that event checkpoint. Those roots remain distinct and keep that snapshot throughout this one batch. A body which subsequently ceases to exist receives no fabricated packet, and an unused root creates no effect/resource. For single-target events create the root at that damage checkpoint. Deep Crust Spike's two types and Soul Pyre's two types stay one event/root for that recipient. The wall's one collapse is one event per admitted recipient. Molecular Destabilization and Dimensional Break each have one force event. Incendiary Cloud creates fresh original-caster roots for the separately authorized pulse's admitted recipients before any of that pulse's consequences, expressly reevaluating that caster's current unsuppressed Null flag at each pulse; it is not one inherited whole-cloud root. This mapping does not alter Spectral Army, Mental Prison or any other source's root policy.
A root under unsuppressed Null Veil retains that flag in its typed components and pure transfers. Later Veil changes do not rewrite an existing root. Different recipients/casts/pulses remain distinct even if sharing one raw pool. Resolve real typed mitigation/prevention and source-specific sharing at their actual stages, then temp HP, normal HP and accepted lifecycle once per terminal recipient. A post-defense transfer that prohibits repeat mitigation or derived recovery retains those restrictions. A redirected damage portion never moves the spell's original rider to the new damage recipient. ActualDamageDealt is temp HP absorbed plus normal HP lost, not overkill; none of these spells creates recovery, soul custody, capture permission or resources merely by dealing damage.
For an ordinary living damage event, evaluate Instant Death using post-temp remainder versus pre-event HP and effective maximum; if not instantly dead, actual positive-to-zero or at-least-half-maximum damage requires the real Wound save (CON DC max(10,floor(post-temp damage/2)), with existing Wound penalties), without inventing Maiming. Actual zero-HP and Wound/death replacements complete before a later spell rider. Null living branches suppress this damage's Instant Death and Wound/Maiming save; positive-to-zero establishes Stable0 under its actual recovery clock, while existing Dying0 and Stable0 keep their distinct recorded states/clocks. Actual finite or nonliving recipients use their own reviewed lifecycle, not a generic guessed anatomy. Costs, corpse consumption, source expiry and concentration endings remain non-damage consequences under Null.
Damage to a concentrating creature still invokes its actual concentration procedure, DC max(10,floor(resolved damage/2)), including real temp-HP absorption. Separate later Falling is its own ordinary consequence; no fictional crush or immediate landing packet is attached. All same-event class/passive hooks require their real acquisition, timing and eligibility. Re-evaluate ongoing source validity after response/concentration/life processing before a pending ongoing component establishes.
## RD8-MIXED-02
NEW RD8-MIXED-02 applies ONLY to Deep Crust Spike, Soul Pyre and actually authorized descendants of those packets. It does not grant a reduction or change Meteor Spear/Ice Storm. Roll the explicitly partitioned pools once; apply the spell's save factor independently to each type with floor. Select one strongest competing class flat reduction for the whole event under CLASS_PLAYTEST_RULES, not one reduction before resistance plus another afterward. If eligible competing amounts tie and no more specific source selects, use source-card/mechanical ID, then original owner ID, then application ID, lexical and fixed before mitigation outcomes. This explicitly chosen tie key follows the complete Ice Storm precedent rather than silently replacing Meteor's different key. A selected ordinary class reduction acts before typed resistance; Spinal Plating acts after resistance only if it is the selected actually acquired active eligible source. A single-target Deep Crust Spike is not an area merely because its eruption has an origin; area-only Spinal Plating does not qualify there.
At the selected aggregate stage combine like canonical types. For nonnegative eligible amounts a_i, A=sum(a_i), allocate B=min(actual reduction,A). If A=0 allocate nothing. Assign floor(B*a_i/A), then distribute remaining integer points by greatest exact fractional remainder and lexical canonical damage-type ID ties. Zero/ineligible types receive no share, no type goes negative, and later immunity/resistance cannot be used to optimize the prior allocation. Apply each actual resistance/immunity and rounding once at its proper stage. Retain typed residuals through any source-qualified transfer; a post-mitigation transfer is not mitigated again. Finish one Core/lifecycle/concentration event per terminal recipient, not one per damage type.
## RD8-INFORMATION-02
NEW express cohort adoption of P20's private RNG/information boundary. Unless a procedure specifies otherwise, one raw damage pool (or one pair of typed pools) is shared by all admitted recipients of the same area event; each has its own save, defenses and root. A single-target spell rolls its one recipient pool. A cloud pulse rolls anew only if it has at least one admitted eligible exposure; a wholly suppressed/no-recipient pulse is recorded as missed without manufacturing a damage roll. Conditional later branches draw only when genuinely required. Native faces, totals, eligibility identities, random seeds and future draws remain private engine evidence. Authored worked-case dice are supplied fixtures, not player-visible random oracles. No sixth DE4/Fate presentation, hidden recipient roster, immunity diagnostic, secret origin plane or successful-save report is granted.
## RD8-ENDINGS-02
Apply actual named suppressors and removal sources, not generic antimagic by name. Rank-8 spells are outside Globe's rank-0–5 boundary; Globe does not stop them on that basis. Lesser Antimagic has no rank ceiling: validate its actual source region, line of effect and expression location. A legal cast inside suppression may pay and then fail to manifest; casting is not retroactively free. An instantaneous event blocked at its actual manifestation/original-recipient locus produces no postponed payload on later emergence. Already completed damage, movement and corpse consumption are not undone by a later dispel. Real changed objects and gear remain real.
The cloud and surviving wall image have original establishment+60-second concentration deadlines, with expiry winning at equality; suppression does not pause them or create a new pulse/collapse. Partial field suppression removes only the actual intersecting expression under the suppressor; it does not erase an out-of-field region or prevent an already remote field merely because its caster later enters. Full generating-source removal or concentration loss ends remaining expression. Dimensional Break's established exile instead uses the expressly adopted permanent closure on actual application suppression/removal. Source-body/caster death or incapacitation follows actual concentration rules, not an invented remote cancellation predicate. Greater Dispel Magic keeps generating rank 8 and its actual high-rank check DC26; a differently scoped Wizard Dispel Magic class card keeps its own DC18 and costs. No generic spell sharing, reset or returned material is created by cleanup.

          "completeActualProcedure": "Summon Corpse Golem remains Necromancy rank 4, Action, 60 feet, base 7 RP, V/S/M-F and concentration up to one minute. Its printed HP60, 2d10 slam and slow aura all survive. NEW this is one generated temporary creature, not resurrection or assembly from actual corpse inventory. The name and material/focus alternative do not impose a corpse, soul, blood or unprinted price. Declare a perceived, supported, unoccupied Large 10x10 whole-body space within 60 feet and line of effect and bind its measured height before commitment. Pay the actual spell price and start concentration normally. Successful formation starts the same body's absolute 60-second deadline.\n\nNEW complete bounded combat profile: Large mechanical Construct, base HP60, AC15, mass600 lb, walk20 feet, ordinary sight/hearing, no special vision. All six saves are original creating source-owner PB as the total bonus; other default untrained checks are +0. These are the inherited missing-stat fallback, not permission to invent a high attribute/proficiency. Two manipulators can carry genuinely fitting noncreature gear subject to a local maximum40 lb AND4Load, available hands and real fit. It has no fine artisan use, skill/tool proficiency, spell/resource pool, resistance, immunity, regeneration or extra natural weapon by type/name. Actual body height and needed environmental traits must be supplied in a future geometry/exposure fixture, not guessed from a two-foot box.\n\nAt its one legally commanded attached phase choose exactly one of two offensive Actions. Slam: one melee attack against an actually perceived target within 5 feet and LoE from the Golem's body, using original owner's current creating-class spell attack, dealing 2d10 bludgeoning with no ability add; attack critical uses 4d10. A true shared allowance must remain for this Golem, not another owned asset.\n\nSlow Aura is the other offensive Action, not a free passive in addition to Slam. Establish a 10-foot radius centered on this same moving body with actual origin LoE, ending at the original source owner's next future regular START. At activation declare the finite actual enemy identities selected from opposition known to that owner/body; this supplies no hidden hostility detector or new information. Each selected creature already inside, or entering before that endpoint, makes one CON save against the actual casting DC snapshotted when the aura is activated. Success excludes that creature from this activation. Failure halves its Speed only while its body is within the live aura/LoE, under strongest-adverse-Speed arbitration. Leaving removes this aura's active modifier; re-entry restores that same failure without a second save. A later genuinely commanded activation has its own single-save record. The aura grants no Action loss, full Slow condition, hard-control claim, new attack or duplicated same-name slow. Body disappearance, incapacity where actual aura rules require consciousness, or suppression disables the aura; its original endpoint keeps running and it does not queue a later application.\n\nThere is one live Corpse Golem source per original caster. Formation does not itself attack; an available real creation-turn Bonus Action may command through CL10-COMMAND-01. Ordinary zero-HP destruction, expiry, concentration ending, actual removal or full sustaining-expression suppression ends it and the source's remaining aura, without corpse/XP/loot/harvest. Qualifying Null instead leaves the same extant body NullDisabled0 while the source persists; no dying/Wound/Stable engine is added and no generic organic-healing repair is granted. Independent ending/suppression still removes that disabled body. Real external cargo settles at the current actual location under legal item placement; intrinsic appearance creates no material stock.",

              "completeActualProcedure": "Summon Corpse Golem remains Necromancy rank 4, Action, 60 feet, base 7 RP, V/S/M-F and concentration up to one minute. Declare a perceived, supported, unoccupied Large 10x10 whole-body space within 60 feet and line of effect and bind its measured height before commitment. Pay the actual spell price and start concentration normally. Successful formation starts the same body's absolute 60-second deadline.\n\nNEW complete bounded combat profile: Large mechanical Construct, base HP60, AC15, mass600 lb, walk20 feet, ordinary sight/hearing, no special vision. All six saves are original creating source-owner PB as the total bonus; other default untrained checks are +0. These are the inherited missing-stat fallback, not permission to invent a high attribute/proficiency. Two manipulators can carry genuinely fitting noncreature gear subject to a local maximum40 lb AND4Load, available hands and real fit. It has no fine artisan use, skill/tool proficiency, spell/resource pool, resistance, immunity, regeneration or extra natural weapon by type/name. Actual body height and needed environmental traits must be supplied in a future geometry/exposure fixture, not guessed from a two-foot box.\n\nAt its one legally commanded attached phase choose exactly one of two offensive Actions. Slam: one melee attack against an actually perceived target within 5 feet and LoE from the Golem's body, using original owner's current creating-class spell attack, dealing 2d10 bludgeoning with no ability add; attack critical uses 4d10. A true shared allowance must remain for this Golem, not another owned asset.\n\nSlow Aura is the other offensive Action, not a free passive in addition to Slam. Establish a 10-foot radius centered on this same moving body with actual origin LoE, ending at the original source owner's next future regular START. At activation declare the finite actual enemy identities selected from opposition known to that owner/body; this supplies no hidden hostility detector or new information. Each selected creature already inside, or entering before that endpoint, makes one CON save against the actual casting DC snapshotted when the aura is activated. Success excludes that creature from this activation. Failure halves its Speed only while its body is within the live aura/LoE, under strongest-adverse-Speed arbitration. Leaving removes this aura's active modifier; re-entry restores that same failure without a second save. A later genuinely commanded activation has its own single-save record. The aura grants no Action loss, full Slow condition, hard-control claim, new attack or duplicated same-name slow. Body disappearance, incapacity where actual aura rules require consciousness, or suppression disables the aura; its original endpoint keeps running and it does not queue a later application.\n\nThere is one live Corpse Golem source per original caster. Formation does not itself attack; an available real creation-turn Bonus Action may command through CL10-COMMAND-01. Ordinary zero-HP destruction, expiry, concentration ending, actual removal or full sustaining-expression suppression ends it and the source's remaining aura, without corpse/XP/loot/harvest. Qualifying Null instead leaves the same extant body NullDisabled0 while the source persists; no dying/Wound/Stable engine is added and no generic organic-healing repair is granted. Independent ending/suppression still removes that disabled body.               "completeActualProcedure": "Summon Corpse Golem remains Necromancy rank 4, Action, 60 feet, base 7 RP, V/S/M-F and concentration up to one minute. Its printed HP60, 2d10 slam and slow aura all survive. NEW this is one generated temporary creature, not resurrection or assembly from actual corpse inventory. The name and material/focus alternative do not impose a corpse, soul, blood or unprinted price. Declare a perceived, supported, unoccupied Large 10x10 whole-body space within 60 feet and line of effect and bind its measured height before commitment. Pay the actual spell price and start concentration normally. Successful formation starts the same body's absolute 60-second deadline.\n\nNEW complete bounded combat profile: Large mechanical Construct, base HP60, AC15, mass600 lb, walk20 feet, ordinary sight/hearing, no special vision. All six saves are original creating source-owner PB as the total bonus; other default untrained checks are +0. These are the inherited missing-stat fallback, not permission to invent a high attribute/proficiency. Two manipulators can carry genuinely fitting noncreature gear subject to a local maximum40 lb AND4Load, available hands and real fit. It has no fine artisan use, skill/tool proficiency, spell/resource pool, resistance, immunity, regeneration or extra natural weapon by type/name. Actual body height and needed environmental traits must be supplied in a future geometry/exposure fixture, not guessed from a two-foot box.\n\nAt its one legally commanded attached phase choose exactly one of two offensive Actions. Slam: one melee attack against an actually perceived target within 5 feet and LoE from the Golem's body, using original owner's current creating-class spell attack, dealing 2d10 bludgeoning with no ability add; attack critical uses 4d10. A true shared allowance must remain for this Golem, not another owned asset.\n\nSlow Aura is the other offensive Action, not a free passive in addition to Slam. Establish a 10-foot radius centered on this same moving body with actual origin LoE, ending at the original source owner's next future regular START. At activation declare the finite actual enemy identities selected from opposition known to that owner/body; this supplies no hidden hostility detector or new information. Each selected creature already inside, or entering before that endpoint, makes one CON save against the actual casting DC snapshotted when the aura is activated. Success excludes that creature from this activation. Failure halves its Speed only while its body is within the live aura/LoE, under strongest-adverse-Speed arbitration. Leaving removes this aura's active modifier; re-entry restores that same failure without a second save. A later genuinely commanded activation has its own single-save record. The aura grants no Action loss, full Slow condition, hard-control claim, new attack or duplicated same-name slow. Body disappearance, incapacity where actual aura rules require consciousness, or suppression disables the aura; its original endpoint keeps running and it does not queue a later application.\n\nThere is one live Corpse Golem source per original caster. Formation does not itself attack; an available real creation-turn Bonus Action may command through CL10-COMMAND-01. Ordinary zero-HP destruction, expiry, concentration ending, actual removal or full sustaining-expression suppression ends it and the source's remaining aura, without corpse/XP/loot/harvest. Qualifying Null instead leaves the same extant body NullDisabled0 while the source persists; no dying/Wound/Stable engine is added and no generic organic-healing repair is granted. Independent ending/suppression still removes that disabled body. Real external cargo settles at the current actual location under legal item placement; intrinsic appearance creates no material stock.",
